- **classifier-author**: Drafts a builtin classifier for ONE novel permanent-limitation group. Studies the false-positive pattern across the group's sample triage entries, then writes three staging artifacts — draft_entry.json (a complete KnownIssue), check_&lt;group_id&gt;.ts (a BuiltinCheckFn), and REVIEW.md (human apply steps). Never writes registry.json; the human reviews the staging dir and applies via reconcile-registry --stage. ([CRJFisher/ariadne](https://github.com/CRJFisher/ariadne/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/classifier-author.md) · ★23)
- **classifier-fixer**: Repairs ONE drifted rule's builtin classifier from its drift_evidence[]. Runs the fixability triage on every evidence case first, then terminates each adjudicated case as captured (predicate broadened, positive + negative-guard tests), fixable-in-Ariadne (backlog-task proposal, never absorbed), or mis-classified (hand-off toward classifier-author). Edits only check_&lt;group_id&gt;.ts and its test; never writes registry.json. ([CRJFisher/ariadne](https://github.com/CRJFisher/ariadne/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/classifier-fixer.md) · ★23)

- **claude-code**: - Never run a plain `ctx7 setup`: it writes the context7 key into `~/.claude.json`. `ctx7 setup --claude --oauth` restores ctx7's skill and rule but replaces the context7 MCP entry with a keyless one, so after such a run the owner restores it with macos-setup's `./setup.sh context7`. - Machine specifics: the `my-machine-configs` skill, `references/claude-code.md`. ([Tapppi/dotfiles](https://github.com/Tapppi/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/agents/claude-code.md) · ★1)
